## Activations

### batchQuery

`activations.batchQuery`

Batch Query Activations for Space. Lists Activations in bulk across many Audiences in one call. Intended for bulk reads, for example pulling activation metadata for hundreds of audiences on a schedule, so callers don't need one listActivationsFromAudience call per audience. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.activations.batchQuery({ spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `audienceIds` | `string[]` | No | Audience IDs to list activations for. Max 100 IDs per call. Duplicates are de-duplicated server-side but still count toward the cap. Omit (or pass an empty array) to scan every audience in the space instead - see the `pagination` doc below for how that changes what a "page" means. Omitting this list is materially higher latency per call than a scoped batch, since each page has to enumerate audiences before it can look up their activations. |
| `pagination` | `object` | No | Optional pagination. When `audienceIds` is provided, this paginates the combined activation result set across those audiences (`count` = activations per page). When `audienceIds` is omitted, this paginates the space's *audiences* instead, fully expanding each page's activations (`count` = audiences per page, not activations per page) - so response size per page is not directly bounded by `count` in that mode. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="pagination full type">

```ts
{
  cursor?: string,
  count: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output for listing activations in bulk across many audiences. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  activations?: {
    id?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    workspaceId?: string | null,
    spaceId?: string | null,
    audienceId?: string | null,
    connectionId?: string | null,
    activationType?: string | null,
    activationName?: string | null,
    displayName?: string | null | null,
    personalization?: {
      profile?: {
        properties?: string[] | null,
        mapping?: Record<string, string> | null
      } | null,
      entities?: {
        properties?: string[] | null,
        relationshipSlug?: string | null
      }[] | null,
      syncEntityPropertyChanges?: boolean | null
    } | null,
    destinationMapping?: {
      actionId?: string | null,
      settings?: {
      } | null
    } | null,
    performResync?: boolean | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    updatedAt?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  errors?: {
    audienceId?: string | null,
    message?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  pagination?: {
    current?: string | null,
    next?: string | null | null,
    previous?: string | null | null,
    totalEntries?: number | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### create

`activations.create`

Add Activation to Audience. Defines how audience data is sent to a Destination. This step enables the data sync. You must establish a connection (Add Destination to Audience) for this audience before an Activation can be created. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.activations.create({
	spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW',
	audienceId: 'example',
	connectionId: 'example',
	performResync: true,
	activationType: 'example',
	activationName: 'example',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `audienceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `connectionId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Determines whether an activation is enabled. |
| `performResync` | `boolean` | Yes | Determines whether to perform a full resync upon creation. If true, the entire audience is resent to the Destination from scratch. If false, only future changes will be synced. |
| `activationType` | `string` | Yes | Determines when an event is sent to the Destination. Possible values: - **Audience Entered**: Sends an event when a profile or entity enters the audience. - **Audience Exited**: Sends an event when a profile or entity exits the audience. - **Audience Membership Changed**: Sends an event for both entries and exits. This does not apply to entities. - **Entity Added**: Sends an event when an entity is added to the audience. - **Entity Removed**: Sends an event when an entity is removed from the audience. - **Entity Values Changed**: Sends an event when an entity's property values change. Restricted to freshness-enabled Destinations. - **All Events**: Sends events for all trigger conditions (enters, exits, and entity value changes for freshness Destinations; enters and exits only for non-freshness Destinations). Note that events are sent for the profile, unless the audience is a Linked Audience. In that case, events are sent for the target entity defined for that audience. Activations on Warehouse Destinations support only track-based activation types. **Audience Membership Changed** is identify-based, so requests using it for a Warehouse Destination are rejected. Use **Audience Entered** or **Audience Exited** instead. |
| `activationName` | `string` | Yes | Activation name. For Warehouse Destinations, this is used as the table name. |
| `displayName` | `string` | No | Optional human-readable label for the activation. Only supported for Warehouse Destinations. When omitted, the activationName is used as the label. |
| `personalization` | `object` | No | The data points used to enrich the event. Defines which profile traits and/or entity properties are included in the event sent to the Destination. For Action Destinations, any traits or properties specified here must also be included in the destinationMapping to define which Destination fields should be populated. May be omitted to create an activation without personalization, in which case the created activation is returned with no profile properties and no entities. |
| `destinationMapping` | `object` | No | Defines the specific action and data mapping for the Destination. Only applicable for Action Destinations. Action id: Specifies which action to perform on the Destination (for example: add contact, update list). Settings/Mapping: Defines how event data (including personalization traits) populates specific fields in the Destination. Use the List Supported Destinations from Audience endpoint to find available action ids and the specific Destination fields you can map. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="personalization full type">

```ts
{
  profile?: {
    properties?: string[],
    mapping?: Record<string, string>
  },
  entities?: {
    properties: string[],
    relationshipSlug: string
  }[],
  syncEntityPropertyChanges?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="destinationMapping full type">

```ts
{
  actionId: string,
  settings?: {
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Activation output for create. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  activation?: {
    id?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    workspaceId?: string | null,
    spaceId?: string | null,
    audienceId?: string | null,
    connectionId?: string | null,
    activationType?: string | null,
    activationName?: string | null,
    displayName?: string | null | null,
    personalization?: {
      profile?: {
        properties?: string[] | null,
        mapping?: Record<string, string> | null
      } | null,
      entities?: {
        properties?: string[] | null,
        relationshipSlug?: string | null
      }[] | null,
      syncEntityPropertyChanges?: boolean | null
    } | null,
    destinationMapping?: {
      actionId?: string | null,
      settings?: {
      } | null
    } | null,
    performResync?: boolean | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    updatedAt?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### delete

`activations.delete`

Remove Activation from Audience. Deletes an Activation. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.activations.delete({ spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW', audienceId: 'example', id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `audienceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output for removing an activation from audience. |

```ts
  status?: string | null
} | null
```

### update

`activations.update`

Update Activation for Audience. Updates an Activation. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.activations.update({ spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW', audienceId: 'example', id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `audienceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Determines whether an activation is enabled. |
| `activationName` | `string` | No | Activation name. For Warehouse Destinations, this is used as the table name. Omit this field to leave the name unchanged. An empty string is ignored rather than applied. |
| `displayName` | `string \| null` | No | Optional human-readable label for the activation. Only supported for Warehouse Destinations. Send null to clear. When omitted, the activationName is used as the label. |
| `personalization` | `object` | No | The data points used to enrich the event. Defines which profile traits and/or entity properties are included in the event sent to the Destination. For Action Destinations, any traits or properties specified here must also be included in the destinationMapping to define which Destination fields should be populated. On update, profile and entities are evaluated separately. Omit either one to leave it unchanged. A value that is supplied replaces the stored value outright instead of being combined with it, so include every trait or property that should remain. Supplying an empty entities array removes all entities. |
| `destinationMapping` | `object` | No | Defines the specific action and data mapping for the Destination. Only applicable for Action Destinations. Action id: Specifies which action to perform on the Destination (for example: add contact, update list). Settings/Mapping: Defines how event data (including personalization traits) populates specific fields in the Destination. Use the List Supported Destinations from Audience endpoint to find available action ids and the specific Destination fields you can map. On update, settings are applied one field at a time. A field that is not supplied keeps its stored value, and a field that is supplied has its value replaced outright. |
| `performResync` | `boolean` | No | Determines whether to perform a full resync after the update. If true, the entire audience is resent to the Destination using the updated configuration. If false, the update applies only to future syncs. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="personalization full type">

```ts
{
  profile?: {
    properties?: string[],
    mapping?: Record<string, string>
  },
  entities?: {
    properties: string[],
    relationshipSlug: string
  }[],
  syncEntityPropertyChanges?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="destinationMapping full type">

```ts
{
  actionId: string,
  settings?: {
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output for updating an activation for audience. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  activation?: {
    id?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    workspaceId?: string | null,
    spaceId?: string | null,
    audienceId?: string | null,
    connectionId?: string | null,
    activationType?: string | null,
    activationName?: string | null,
    displayName?: string | null | null,
    personalization?: {
      profile?: {
        properties?: string[] | null,
        mapping?: Record<string, string> | null
      } | null,
      entities?: {
        properties?: string[] | null,
        relationshipSlug?: string | null
      }[] | null,
      syncEntityPropertyChanges?: boolean | null
    } | null,
    destinationMapping?: {
      actionId?: string | null,
      settings?: {
      } | null
    } | null,
    performResync?: boolean | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    updatedAt?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```

</AccordionGroup>

## Audiences

### create

`audiences.create`

Create Audience. Creates Audience. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Audience feature enabled.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.audiences.create({
	spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW',
	name: 'example',
	definition: { query: 'example' },
	audienceType: 'ACCOUNTS',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | Name of the audience. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Determines whether a computation is enabled. |
| `description` | `string` | No | Description of the audience. |
| `definition` | `object` | Yes | Query language definition. |
| `audienceType` | `ACCOUNTS \| LINKED \| USERS` | Yes | Denotes the type of audience product. Possible values: USERS, ACCOUNTS, LINKED. |
| `options` | `object` | No | Represents the options of Including Anonymous users and Historical Data. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="definition full type">

```ts
{
  targetEntity?: string | null,
  query: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="options full type">

```ts
{
  includeHistoricalData?: boolean,
  filterByExternalIds?: string[],
  backfillEventDataDays?: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Audience output for create. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  audience?: {
    id?: string | null,
    spaceId?: string | null,
    name?: string | null,
    description?: string | null,
    key?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    definition?: {
      targetEntity?: string | null | null,
      query?: string | null
    } | null | null,
    status?: string | null,
    createdBy?: string | null,
    updatedBy?: string | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    updatedAt?: string | null,
    audienceType?: string | null,
    options?: {
      includeHistoricalData?: boolean | null,
      filterByExternalIds?: string[] | null,
      backfillEventDataDays?: number | null
    } | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### createPreview

`audiences.createPreview`

Create Audience Preview. Previews Audience. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Audience feature enabled. This endpoint also has a rate limit of 700 requests per month per spaceId, which is lower than the default due to access pattern restrictions.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.audiences.createPreview({
	spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW',
	definition: { query: 'example' },
	audienceType: 'ACCOUNTS',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `definition` | `object` | Yes | Query language definition. |
| `audienceType` | `ACCOUNTS \| LINKED \| USERS` | Yes | Denotes the type of audience product. Possible values: USERS, ACCOUNTS, LINKED. |
| `options` | `object` | No | Options which should be applied when segmenting audiences. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="definition full type">

```ts
{
  targetEntity?: string | null,
  query: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="options full type">

```ts
{
  filterByExternalIds?: string[],
  backfillEventDataDays?: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output when creating an audience preview. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  audiencePreview?: {
    id?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### destinations.add

`audiences.destinations.add`

Add Destination to Audience. Establishes a connection between an audience and a Destination. To start syncing data, you must create an Activation for the connection created here. Note that the Destination must be added to Engage through the Engage Settings page within the App before creating the connection through the API. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.audiences.destinations.add({
	spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW',
	audienceId: 'example',
	destination: { id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q', type: 'example' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `audienceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `destination` | `object` | Yes | The Destination. |
| `idSyncConfiguration` | `object[]` | No | Identifier sync configuration. Defines which external ids to sync and their selection strategies. Maximum 5 items allowed. If omitted, the default will be last email and last user_id, or all email and all user_id depending on the Destination. |
| `connectionSettings` | `any` | No | The settings that a Destination requires to create audiences on a third-party platform. These settings are Destination-specific. Use the List Supported Destinations from Audience endpoint to find the required connection settings. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="destination full type">

```ts
{
  id: string,
  type: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="idSyncConfiguration full type">

```ts
{
  externalId: string,
  strategy: string,
  mapTo?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  connection?: {
    id?: string | null
  } | null,
  idSyncConfiguration?: {
    externalId?: string | null,
    strategy?: string | null,
    mapTo?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  connectionSettings?: any | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

`audiences.destinations.listSupported`

### destinations.remove

`audiences.destinations.remove`

Remove Destination from Audience. Removes a Destination from an Audience. If there are activations associated with the Destination, the request will return a 409 Conflict error. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Audience feature enabled. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.audiences.destinations.remove({ spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW', audienceId: 'example', destinationId: 'fP7qoQw2HTWt9WdMr718gn' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `audienceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `destinationId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output for removing a Destination from an Audience. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  status?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### destinations.update

`audiences.destinations.update`

Update Destination for Audience. Updates a Destination for an Audience. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Audience feature enabled. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.audiences.destinations.update({ spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW', audienceId: 'example', destinationId: 'fP7qoQw2HTWt9WdMr718gn' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `audienceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `destinationId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idSyncConfiguration` | `object[]` | No | Identifier sync configuration - array of external IDs to sync with their strategies. Maximum 5 items allowed. The supplied array replaces the entire stored configuration, so include every external id that should remain. Omitting mapTo on an entry clears any value stored for it. An empty array is treated the same as omitting the field and leaves the stored configuration unchanged. |
| `connectionSettings` | `any` | No | The settings that a Destination requires to create audiences on a third-party platform. The supplied object replaces the stored settings outright, so include every setting that should remain. Settings that are omitted are removed. An empty object is treated the same as omitting the field and leaves the stored settings unchanged. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="idSyncConfiguration full type">

```ts
{
  externalId: string,
  strategy: string,
  mapTo?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output for updating a Destination for an Audience. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  destination?: {
    id?: string | null
  } | null,
  idSyncConfiguration?: {
    externalId?: string | null,
    strategy?: string | null,
    mapTo?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  connectionSettings?: any | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### run

`audiences.run`

Force Execute Audience Run. The ability to force execute a run for an Audience is limited to Linked Audiences (audienceType = LINKED). In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Audience feature enabled. Recomputes the audience now and sends changes to its connected destinations.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.audiences.run({ spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW', audienceId: 'example' });
```

```ts
    id?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```

### schedules.create

`audiences.schedules.create`

Add Audience Schedule to Audience. The ability to configure the run schedule for an Audience is limited to Linked Audiences (audienceType = LINKED). Note that if a Linked Audience remains disabled for 90 days Segment will delete the associated schedule and a new schedule will need to be created.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.audiences.schedules.create({
	spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW',
	id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q',
	strategy: 'PERIODIC',
	config: { interval: 'example' },
});
```

| `config` | `object \| null` | Yes | Configuration for PERIODIC or SPECIFIC_DAYS strategies. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="config full type">

```ts
} | {

  timezone: string
} | null
```

**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

```ts
{

    } | {
```

**Risk:** `write`

| `definition` | `object` | No | Represents the Compute Query Language definition of the computation and type of computation. |

### list

List Audit Events. Returns a list of Audit Trail events.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.auditTrail.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `startTime` | `string` | No | Filter response to events that happened after this time. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `endTime` | `string` | No | Filter response to events that happened before this time. Defaults to the current time, or the end time from the pagination cursor. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `resourceId` | `string` | No | Filter response to events that affect a specific resource, for example, a single Source. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `resourceType` | `string` | No | Filter response to events that affect a specific type, for example, Sources, Warehouses, and Tracking Plans. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `pagination` | `object` | No | Pagination parameters. Every resource that returns a list of items in its `Output` object may contain a `PaginationInput` in its `Input` object. Required, though some of its fields are optional. |

<Accordion title="pagination full type">

```ts
  count: number
}
```

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Returns a list of Audit Trail events for the current Workspace. |

## Computed Traits

### create

`computedTraits.create`

Create Computed Trait. Creates a Computed Trait. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Computed Trait feature enabled. Note: The definition for a Computed Trait created using the API is not editable through the Segment App. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.computedTraits.create({
	spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW',
	name: 'example',
	definition: { query: 'example', type: 'ACCOUNTS' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The name of the computation. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Determines whether a computation is enabled. |
| `description` | `string` | No | The description of the computation. |
| `definition` | `object` | Yes | Represents the Compute Query Language definition of the computation and type of computation. |
| `options` | `object` | No | Options which should be applied when aggregating computed traits. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="definition full type">

```ts
{
  type: ACCOUNTS | USERS,
  query: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="options full type">

```ts
{
  includeHistoricalData?: boolean,
  includeAnonymousUsers?: boolean,
  backfillDurationDays?: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Computed Trait output for create. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  computedTrait?: {
    id?: string | null,
    spaceId?: string | null,
    name?: string | null,
    description?: string | null,
    key?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    definition?: {
      query?: string | null,
      type?: string | null
    } | null | null,
    status?: string | null,
    createdBy?: string | null,
    updatedBy?: string | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    updatedAt?: string | null,
    options?: {
      includeHistoricalData?: boolean | null,
      includeAnonymousUsers?: boolean | null,
      backfillDurationDays?: number | null
    } | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### update

`computedTraits.update`

Update Computed Trait for Space. Updates the Computed Trait. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Computed Trait feature enabled. Note that when a Computed Trait is updated, the Computed Trait will be locked from future edits until the changes have been incorporated. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.computedTraits.update({ spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW', id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q' });
```

| `description` | `string` | No | The description of the computation. |
| `definition` | `object` | No | Query language definition and type. Note: The definition for a Computed Trait updated using the API is not editable through the Segment App. |

## Credentials

### create

`credentials.create`

Create Credential. Creates a new Credential. This endpoint requires the user to have at least the following permission(s):. * Workspace Owner. * Warehouse Admin. * Source Admin. * Entities Admin. * Unify and Engage Admin.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.credentials.create({
	name: 'example',
	settings: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string` | Yes | A human-readable name for this Credential. |
| `settings` | `object` | Yes | A key-value object that contains type-specific settings for this Credential. `settings.type` is required and determines the Credential type (for example `Snowflake` or `Redshift`). Different types require different settings; see the `settings.type` discriminator in the OpenAPI spec for the full set of required and optional fields per type. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="settings full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Returns the newly created Credential. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  credential?: {
    id?: string | null,
```

### delete

`credentials.delete`

Delete Credential. Deletes an existing Credential. Fails with a 409 Conflict if the Credential is still in use by a Warehouse or Source (including a disabled one). Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.credentials.delete({ credentialId: 'cred_8r5mNkPq2Wm4zR1t' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `credentialId` | `string` | Yes | — |

| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Returns the status of a Credential deletion. |

```ts
} | null
```

### update

`credentials.update`

Update Credential. Updates an existing Credential. All Warehouses using this Credential are affected immediately. This endpoint requires the user to have at least the following permission(s):. * Workspace Owner.

**Risk:** `write`

| `credentialId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `name` | `string` | No | An optional human-readable name to associate with this Credential. |
| `settings` | `object` | No | A key-value object that contains type-specific settings for this Credential. Supports partial updates — only the fields you want to change need to be provided. `settings.type` cannot be changed. |

```ts
}
```

## Delivery Overview

### getFailedDelivery

`deliveryOverview.getFailedDelivery`

Get Egress Failed Metrics from Delivery Overview. Get events that failed to be delivered to Destination. Beta in Segment's API.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.deliveryOverview.getFailedDelivery({
	sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn',
	destinationConfigId: 'example',
	startTime: 'example',
	endTime: 'example',
	granularity: 'DAY',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sourceId` | `string` | Yes | The sourceId for the Workspace. This parameter exists in beta. |
| `destinationConfigId` | `string` | Yes | The id tied to a Workspace Destination. This parameter exists in beta. |
| `startTime` | `string` | Yes | The ISO8601 formatted timestamp corresponding to the beginning of the requested time frame, inclusive. This parameter exists in beta. |
| `endTime` | `string` | Yes | The ISO8601 formatted timestamp corresponding to the end of the requested time frame, noninclusive. This parameter exists in beta. |
| `groupBy` | `string[]` | No | A comma-delimited list of strings representing one or more dimensions to group the result by. Valid options are: `event Name`, `event Type`, `discard Reason`, `app Version`, `subscription Id`, `activationId`, `audienceId`, and `spaceId`. This parameter exists in beta. |
| `granularity` | `DAY \| HOUR \| MINUTE` | Yes | The size of each bucket in the requested window. Based on the granularity chosen, there are restrictions on the time range you can query: **Minute**: - Max time range: 4 hours - Oldest possible start time: 48 hours in the past **Hour**: - Max Time range: 14 days - Oldest possible start time: 30 days in the past **Day**: - Max time range: 30 days - Oldest possible start time: 30 days in the past This parameter exists in beta. |
| `filter` | `object` | No | The `DeliveryOverviewDestinationFilterBy` object is a map of the filterable fields and their values. |
| `pagination` | `object` | No | Pagination parameters. Every resource that returns a list of items in its `Output` object may contain a `PaginationInput` in its `Input` object. Required, though some of its fields are optional. |

```ts
  appVersion?: string[],

  activationId?: string[],
  audienceId?: string[],
  spaceId?: string[]
}
```

<Accordion title="pagination full type">

```ts
  cursor?: string,
  count: number
}
```

| `filter` | `object` | No | The `DeliveryOverviewDestinationFilterBy` object is a map of the filterable fields and their values. |
| `pagination` | `object` | No | Pagination parameters. Every resource that returns a list of items in its `Output` object may contain a `PaginationInput` in its `Input` object. Required, though some of its fields are optional. |

### getSuccessfullyReceived

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.deliveryOverview.getSuccessfullyReceived({
	sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn',

});
```

## Destinations

### create

`destinations.create`

Create Destination. Creates a new Destination.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts

	sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn',
	metadataId: 'example',
	settings: {},
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `sourceId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the Source to connect to this Destination instance. Config API note: analogous to `parent`. |
| `metadataId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the metadata to link to the new Destination. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Whether this Destination should receive data. |
| `name` | `string` | No | Defines the display name of the Destination. Config API note: equal to `displayName`. |
| `settings` | `object` | Yes | An object that contains settings for the Destination based on the "required" and "advanced" settings present in the Destination metadata. Config API note: equal to `config`. |

### getDeliveryMetrics

`destinations.getDeliveryMetrics`

List Delivery Metrics Summary from Destination. Get an event delivery metrics summary from a Destination. Based on the granularity chosen, there are restrictions on the time range you can query:. Minute:. - Max time range: 4 hours. - Oldest possible start time: 48 hours in the past. Hour:. - Max Time range: 7 days. Beta in Segment's API.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.destinations.getDeliveryMetrics({ destinationId: 'fP7qoQw2HTWt9WdMr718gn', sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `destinationId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `sourceId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the Source linked to the Destination. Config API note: analogous to `parent`. This parameter exists in beta. |
| `startTime` | `string` | No | Filter events that happened after this time. Defaults to: - 1 hour ago if granularity is `MINUTE`. - 7 days ago if granularity is `HOUR`. - 30 days ago if granularity is `DAY`. This parameter exists in beta. |
| `endTime` | `string` | No | Filter events that happened before this time. Defaults to now if not set. This parameter exists in beta. |
| `granularity` | `DAY \| HOUR \| MINUTE` | No | The granularity to filter metrics to. Either `MINUTE`, `HOUR` or `DAY`. Defaults to `MINUTE` if not set. This parameter exists in beta. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output to retrieve event delivery metrics summary for a Destination. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  deliveryMetricsSummary?: {
    sourceId?: string | null,
    destinationMetadataId?: string | null,
    metrics?: {
      metricName?: string | null,
      total?: number | null,
      breakdown?: {
        metricName?: string | null,
        value?: number | null
      }[] | null
    }[] | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Events

### getVolume

`events.getVolume`

Get Events Volume from Workspace. Enumerates the Workspace event volumes over time in minute increments.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.events.getVolume({ granularity: 'DAY', startTime: 'example', endTime: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `granularity` | `DAY \| HOUR \| MINUTE` | Yes | The size of each bucket in the requested window. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `startTime` | `string` | Yes | The ISO8601 formatted timestamp that corresponds to the beginning of the requested time frame, inclusive. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `endTime` | `string` | Yes | The ISO8601 formatted timestamp that corresponds to the end of the requested time frame, noninclusive. Segment recommends that you lag queries 1 minute behind clock time to reduce the risk for latency to impact the counts. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `groupBy` | `string[]` | No | A comma-delimited list of strings that represents the dimensions to group the result by. The options are: `eventName`, `eventType` and `source`. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `sourceId` | `string[]` | No | A list of strings which filters the results to the given SourceIds. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `eventName` | `string[]` | No | A list of strings which filters the results to the given EventNames. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `eventType` | `string[]` | No | A list of strings which filters the results to the given EventTypes. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `appVersion` | `string[]` | No | A list of strings which filters the results to the given AppVersions. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `pagination` | `object` | No | Pagination parameters. Every resource that returns a list of items in its `Output` object may contain a `PaginationInput` in its `Input` object. Required, though some of its fields are optional. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="pagination full type">

```ts
{
  cursor?: string,
  count: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

## Filters

### create

`filters.create`

Create Filter for Space. Creates a filter for a space. A space filter applies to events coming from all Sources connected to a space. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Space Filters feature enabled. Beta in Segment's API.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.filters.create({ integrationId: 'example', name: 'example', if: 'example' });
```

| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Whether the filter is enabled. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The name of the filter. |
| `description` | `string` | No | The description of the filter. |
| `if` | `string` | Yes | The "if" statement for a filter. |
| `drop` | `boolean` | No | Whether the event is dropped. |

Update Filter By Id. Updates a filter by id and replaces the existing filter. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Space Filters feature enabled. Beta in Segment's API.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.filters.update({ id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q', integrationId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `integrationId` | `string` | Yes | The Integration id of the resource. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Whether the filter is enabled. |
| `name` | `string` | No | The name of the filter. |
| `description` | `string` | No | The description of the filter. |
| `if` | `string` | No | The "if" statement for a filter. |
| `drop` | `boolean` | No | Whether the event is dropped. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output for UpdateFilterById. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  filter?: {
    id?: string | null,
    workspaceId?: string | null,
    integrationId?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    name?: string | null,
    description?: string | null,
    if?: string | null,
    drop?: boolean | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    updatedAt?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Functions

### create

`functions.create`

Create Function. Creates a Function. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Functions feature enabled.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.functions.create({ code: 'example', displayName: 'example', resourceType: 'DESTINATION' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `code` | `string` | Yes | The Function code. |
| `settings` | `object[]` | No | The list of settings for this Function. |
| `displayName` | `string` | Yes | A display name for this Function. Note that Destination Functions append the Workspace to the display name. |
| `logoUrl` | `string` | No | The URL of the logo for this Function. |
| `resourceType` | `DESTINATION \| INSERT_DESTINATION \| INSERT_SOURCE \| INSERT_TRANSFORMATION \| SOURCE` | Yes | The Function type. Config API note: equal to `type`. |
| `description` | `string` | No | A description for this Function. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="settings full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  label: string,
  description: string,
  type: ARRAY | BOOLEAN | STRING | TEXT_MAP,
  required: boolean,
  sensitive: boolean
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Create a Function. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  function?: {
    id?: string | null,
    resourceType?: string | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    createdBy?: string | null,
    code?: string | null,
    deployedAt?: string | null | null,
    settings?: {
      name?: string | null,
      label?: string | null,
      description?: string | null,
      type?: string | null,
      required?: boolean | null,
      sensitive?: boolean | null
    }[] | null,
    displayName?: string | null,
    description?: string | null,
    logoUrl?: string | null,
    previewWebhookUrl?: string | null,
    batchMaxCount?: number | null,
    catalogId?: string | null,
    isLatestVersion?: boolean | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Invites

### create

`invites.create`

Create Invites. Invites a list of users to join a Workspace. When called, this endpoint may generate one or more of the following audit trail events:* Non-Segment User Invited to Workspace. * Policy Created. * New Segment User Invited to Workspace. Config API omitted fields:. - parent. Sends an invitation email to each address.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.invites.create({
	invites: [{ email: 'ada@example.com' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invites` | `object[]` | Yes | The list of invites. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="invites full type">

```ts
{
  email: string,
  permissions?: {
    roleId: string,
    resources?: {
      id: string,
      type: FUNCTION | SOURCE | SPACE | WAREHOUSE | WORKSPACE
    }[],
    labels?: {
      key: string,
      value: string,
      description?: string
    }[]
  }[]
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Returns the emails of the invited users. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  emails?: string[] | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

## Live Plugins

### create

`livePlugins.create`

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.livePlugins.create({ sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn', code: 'example' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

```ts
{

    id?: string | null,
    sourceId?: string | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    createdBy?: string | null,
    downloadURL?: string | null,
    version?: number | null,
    code?: string | null | null
  } | null
} | null
```

```ts
} | null
```

## Profiles Sync

### create

`profilesSync.create`

Create Profiles Warehouse. Creates a new Profiles Warehouse. WARN : Please be careful to not use this API to link multiple Warehouses to a single Space. This is not officially supported. Although the Warehouse will be linked, it won't show up on the UI and can lead to various other unforeseen issues. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.profilesSync.create({
	spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW',
	metadataId: 'example',
	settings: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `metadataId` | `string` | Yes | The Warehouse metadata to use. |
| `name` | `string` | No | An optional human-readable name for this Warehouse. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Enable to allow this Warehouse to receive data. Defaults to true. |
| `settings` | `object` | Yes | A key-value object that contains instance-specific settings for a Warehouse. Different kinds of Warehouses require different settings. The required and optional settings for a Warehouse are described in the `options` object of the associated Warehouse metadata. You can find the full list of Warehouse metadata and related settings information in the `/catalog/warehouses` endpoint. |
| `schemaName` | `string` | No | The custom schema name that Segment uses on the Warehouse side. The space slug value is default otherwise. The schema name cannot be an existing schema name in the Warehouse. To use an existing schema name, please create a profiles Warehouse through the Segment app UI. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="settings full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Returns the newly created Warehouse. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  profilesWarehouse?: {
    id?: string | null,
    spaceId?: string | null,
    metadata?: {
      id?: string | null,
      name?: string | null,
      slug?: string | null,
      description?: string | null,
      logos?: {
        default?: string | null,
        mark?: string | null | null,
        alt?: string | null | null
      } | null,
      options?: {
        name?: string | null,
        type?: string | null,
        required?: boolean | null,
        description?: string | null,
        defaultValue?: any | null,
        label?: string | null
      }[] | null
    } | null,
    workspaceId?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    settings?: {
    } | null,
    schemaName?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Regulations

### create

`regulations.create`

Create Workspace Regulation. Creates a Workspace-scoped regulation. Config API omitted fields:. - attributes,. - userAgent. Rate limit headers will be updated to reflect regulation-specific limits (tracked separately for Segment-only vs Segment & Destination regulation types):. Starts a deletion or suppression run over the subject ids. It cannot be cancelled and deleted data cannot be recovered; poll regulations.get until overallStatus is FINISHED, PARTIAL_SUCCESS or FAILED.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.regulations.create({
	regulationType: 'DELETE_INTERNAL',
	subjectType: 'OBJECT_ID',
	subjectIds: ['example'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `regulationType` | `DELETE_INTERNAL \| DELETE_ONLY \| SUPPRESS_ONLY \| SUPPRESS_WITH_DELETE \| SUPPRESS_WITH_DELETE_INTERNAL \| UNSUPPRESS` | Yes | The regulation type to create. |
| `subjectType` | `OBJECT_ID \| USER_ID` | Yes | The subject type. Use `objectId` for Cloud Source regulations. |
| `subjectIds` | `string[]` | Yes | The list of `userId` or `objectId` values of the subjects to regulate. Config API note: equal to `parent` but allows an array. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | The output of a create Workspace regulation call. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  regulateId?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### createForCloudSource

`regulations.createForCloudSource`

Create Cloud Source Regulation. Creates a Source-scoped regulation. Please Note: Suppression rules at the Workspace level take precedence over those at the Source level. Starts a deletion run on a cloud-mode source. It cannot be cancelled and deleted data cannot be recovered.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.regulations.createForCloudSource({
	sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn',
	regulationType: 'DELETE_INTERNAL',
	subjectType: 'OBJECT_ID',
	subjectIds: ['example'],
	collection: 'example',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sourceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `regulationType` | `DELETE_INTERNAL \| DELETE_ONLY \| SUPPRESS_ONLY \| SUPPRESS_WITH_DELETE \| SUPPRESS_WITH_DELETE_INTERNAL \| UNSUPPRESS` | Yes | The regulation type to create. |
| `subjectType` | `OBJECT_ID` | Yes | The subject type. Must be `objectId` for Cloud Sources. |
| `subjectIds` | `string[]` | Yes | The list of `userId` or `objectId` values of the subjects to regulate. Config API note: equal to `parent` but allows an array. |
| `collection` | `string` | Yes | The Cloud Source collection to regulate. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | The output of a create Cloud Source regulation call. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  regulateId?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### createForSource

`regulations.createForSource`

Create Source Regulation. Creates a Source-scoped regulation. Please Note: Suppression rules at the Workspace level take precedence over those at the Source level. Starts a deletion or suppression run limited to one source. It cannot be cancelled and deleted data cannot be recovered.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.regulations.createForSource({
	sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn',
	regulationType: 'DELETE_ARCHIVE_ONLY',
	subjectType: 'ANONYMOUS_ID',
	subjectIds: ['example'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sourceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `regulationType` | `DELETE_ARCHIVE_ONLY \| DELETE_INTERNAL \| DELETE_ONLY \| SUPPRESS_ONLY \| SUPPRESS_WITH_DELETE \| SUPPRESS_WITH_DELETE_INTERNAL \| UNSUPPRESS` | Yes | The regulation type to create. Please note that `DELETE_ARCHIVE_ONLY` is only supported for limited Workspaces for Source-scoped regulations. |
| `subjectType` | `ANONYMOUS_ID \| USER_ID` | Yes | The subject type. Note: `ANONYMOUS_ID` is only supported for limited Workspaces for Source-scoped regulations. `ANONYMOUS_ID` is only supported when regulationType is `DELETE_ARCHIVE_ONLY`. |
| `subjectIds` | `string[]` | Yes | The list of `userId` or `objectId` or `anonymousId` values of the subjects to regulate. Config API note: equal to `parent` but allows an array. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | The output of a create Source regulation call. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  regulateId?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### list

`regulations.list`

List Workspace Regulations. Lists all Workspace-scoped regulations. Please note: List Workspace regulations only returns deletion requests from the past 90 days. Deletion requests older than 90 days are not retained and will result in 404 resource not found.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.regulations.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `status` | `FAILED \| FINISHED \| INITIALIZED \| INVALID \| NOT_SUPPORTED \| PARTIAL_SUCCESS \| RUNNING` | No | The status on which to filter the returned regulations. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `regulationTypes` | `(DELETE_INTERNAL \| DELETE_ONLY \| SUPPRESS_ONLY \| SUPPRESS_WITH_DELETE \| SUPPRESS_WITH_DELETE_INTERNAL \| UNSUPPRESS)[]` | No | The regulation types on which to filter returned regulations. This parameter exists in v1. |
| `pagination` | `object` | No | Pagination parameters. Every resource that returns a list of items in its `Output` object may contain a `PaginationInput` in its `Input` object. Required, though some of its fields are optional. |

<Accordion title="pagination full type">

```ts
{
  cursor?: string,
  count: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output of all Workspace-scoped regulations in a given Workspace. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  regulations?: {
    id?: string | null,
    subjectType?: string | null,
    subjects?: string[] | null,
    status?: string | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    regulationType?: string | null,
    finishedAt?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  pagination?: {
    current?: string | null,
    next?: string | null | null,
    previous?: string | null | null,
    totalEntries?: number | null
  } | null
} | null
```

**Input**

## Reverse Etl

### models.create

`reverseEtl.models.create`

Create Reverse Etl Model. Creates a new Reverse ETL Model. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.reverseEtl.models.create({
	sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn',
	name: 'example',
	description: 'example',
	enabled: true,
	query: 'example',
	queryIdentifierColumn: 'example',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sourceId` | `string` | Yes | Indicates which Source to attach this model to. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | A short, human-readable description of the Model. |
| `description` | `string` | Yes | A longer, more descriptive explanation of the Model. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | Yes | Indicates whether the Model should have syncs enabled. When disabled, no syncs will be triggered, regardless of the enabled status of the attached destinations/subscriptions. |
| `query` | `string` | Yes | The SQL query that will be executed to extract data from the connected Source. |
| `queryIdentifierColumn` | `string` | Yes | Indicates the column named in `query` that should be used to uniquely identify the extracted records. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Defines the results of creating a Model. |

<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  reverseEtlModel?: {
    id?: string | null,
    sourceId?: string | null,
    name?: string | null,
    description?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    query?: string | null,
    queryIdentifierColumn?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### syncs.cancel

`reverseEtl.syncs.cancel`

Cancel Reverse ETL Sync for Model. Cancels a sync for a Reverse ETL Connection. It might take a few seconds to completely cancel the sync. Will return an error if the sync is already completed or cancelled. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.reverseEtl.syncs.cancel({ modelId: 'example', syncId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `modelId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `syncId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `reasonForCanceling` | `number` | No | The reason for canceling the sync. - IncorrectModel = 0 - IncorrectDest = 1 - IncorrectKeys = 2 - IncorrectMapping = 3 - Other = 4 |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | CancelReverseETLSyncForModelOutput either will return an error or a "CANCELLING" status. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  modelId?: string | null,
  syncId?: string | null,
  errorCode?: string | null,
  errorMessage?: string | null,
  status?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### syncs.create

`reverseEtl.syncs.create`

Create Reverse ETL Manual Sync. Triggers a manual sync for a Reverse ETL Connection. In the request body, the subscription id is the id that follows after /mappings/ portion in the URL of the sync. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change. Starts a sync now: rows go out to the connected destination.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.reverseEtl.syncs.create({ sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn', modelId: 'example', subscriptionId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sourceId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the Source. |
| `modelId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the Model. |
| `subscriptionId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the Subscription. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Output for triggering a manual sync for a RETL connection. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  reverseETLManualSync?: {
    startedAt?: string | null,
    syncId?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### update

`sources.update`

Update Source. Updates an existing Source. When called, this endpoint may generate one or more of the following audit trail events:* Source Modified. * Source Enabled. * Source Settings Modified. * Source Disabled. Config API omitted fields:. - updateMask.

| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Enable to allow the Source to send data. |
| `slug` | `string` | No | The slug that identifies the Source. Config API note: equal to `name`. |
| `settings` | `object` | No | A key-value object that contains instance-specific settings for the Source. Different kinds of Sources require different kinds of input. The settings input for a Source comes from the `options` object associated with this instance of a Source. You can find the full list of required settings by accessing the Sources catalog endpoint under `/catalog/sources`. |

### schema.listTraits

`spaces.schema.listTraits`

List Traits. Returns a list of Traits for a Space. Forward-only pagination : this endpoint does not support backward traversal. The pagination.previous field is always absent; use pagination.next to advance through pages. Alpha in Segment's API: it may change.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.spaces.schema.listTraits({ spaceId: 'spa_9aQ1Lj62S4bomZKLF4DPqW' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `spaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `pagination` | `object` | No | Pagination parameters. Every resource that returns a list of items in its `Output` object may contain a `PaginationInput` in its `Input` object. Required, though some of its fields are optional. |
| `sortBy` | `lastSeenAt \| trait` | No | Field to sort by. Defaults to 'trait'. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `sortDir` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort direction. Defaults to 'asc'. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `search` | `string` | No | Filter traits by key substring. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `collection` | `accounts \| users` | No | Collection to list traits for. Defaults to 'users'. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `includeSampleValues` | `boolean` | No | When true, include sample values for each trait. Defaults to false. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `samplesCount` | `number` | No | Max number of sample values to return per trait. Defaults to 20, min 1, max 100. This parameter exists in alpha. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="pagination full type">

```ts
{
  cursor?: string,
  count: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Response for the listTraits endpoint. |

```ts
  traits?: {
    trait?: string | null,

    lastSeenAt?: string | null,

    next?: string | null | null,

    totalEntries?: number | null
  } | null
```

## Testing

### echo

`testing.echo`

Echo. Public Echo endpoint. (GET /echo)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.testing.echo({ message: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `message` | `string` | Yes | Sets the response `message` field. The response contains this field's entry. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `delay` | `number` | No | The desired response delay, in milliseconds. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `triggerError` | `boolean` | No | If `true`, returns an HTTP `4xx` error that contains the string in `message`. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `triggerMultipleErrors` | `boolean` | No | If `true`, returns an HTTP `4xx` error that contains the value of the `message` field in the error message array. This has no effect if the request sets `triggerError`. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `triggerUnexpectedError` | `boolean` | No | If `true`, triggers a `500` error. This has no effect if the request sets either `triggerError` or `triggerMultipleErrors`. This parameter exists in alpha. |
| `statusCode` | `number` | No | Sets the HTTP status code to return. This parameter exists in alpha. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Echo response. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  method?: string | null,
  message?: string | null,
  headers?: {
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Tracking

### alias

`tracking.alias`

Merge two user identities. Merges two identities of one user (a previous anonymous or old id and the new userId) in destinations that need it, such as Mixpanel. Most destinations merge on identify and do not need alias. Tracking API: sent with the source write key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.tracking.alias({ userId: 'user-1234', previousId: 'anon-5678' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `userId` | `string` | Yes | Your id for the user. Send userId, anonymousId or both. |
| `anonymousId` | `string` | No | A pseudo-unique id for a user you have not identified yet (a session or device id). Send userId, anonymousId or both. |
| `context` | `object` | No | Extra information about the call: ip, userAgent, locale, library, page, campaign, device and so on. Context fields Segment reads are listed in the Common fields spec. |
| `integrations` | `object` | No | Turns destinations on or off for this call: `{ "All": false, "Mixpanel": true }`. |
| `timestamp` | `string` | No | When the event happened, ISO 8601. Left out, Segment uses the time it received the call. Use it when you send historical data. |
| `messageId` | `string` | No | A unique id for the message; Segment drops repeats of the same messageId. Left out, Segment makes one. |
| `previousId` | `string` | Yes | The id the user had before. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="context full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="integrations full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | True when Segment accepted the call. |



---

### batch

`tracking.batch`

Send a batch of calls. Sends up to 500 KB of identify, track, page, screen, group and alias calls in one request (each message at most 32 KB). Use it to import historical data or to save requests. Tracking API: sent with the source write key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.tracking.batch({
	batch: [{ type: 'identify' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `batch` | `object[]` | Yes | The calls, each with its `type`. |
| `context` | `object` | No | Context merged into every message of the batch. |
| `integrations` | `object` | No | Destination switches applied to every message of the batch. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="batch full type">

```ts
{
  type: identify | track | page | screen | group | alias,
  userId?: string,
  anonymousId?: string,
  context?: {
  },
  integrations?: {
  },
  timestamp?: string,
  messageId?: string,
  event?: string,
  name?: string,
  properties?: {
  },
  traits?: {
  },
  groupId?: string,
  previousId?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="context full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="integrations full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | True when Segment accepted the call. |

### identify

`tracking.identify`

Identify a user. Ties a user to their actions and records traits about them (email, name, plan). Traits stay on the user profile in destinations that keep one. Tracking API: sent with the source write key to api.segment.io (events.eu1.segmentapis.com in the EU). Segment answers 200 for every call it can parse; check the source debugger for what arrived.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.tracking.identify({
	userId: 'user-1234',
	traits: { plan: 'pro', name: 'Ada Lovelace' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `userId` | `string` | No | Your id for the user. Send userId, anonymousId or both. |
| `anonymousId` | `string` | No | A pseudo-unique id for a user you have not identified yet (a session or device id). Send userId, anonymousId or both. |
| `context` | `object` | No | Extra information about the call: ip, userAgent, locale, library, page, campaign, device and so on. Context fields Segment reads are listed in the Common fields spec. |
| `integrations` | `object` | No | Turns destinations on or off for this call: `{ "All": false, "Mixpanel": true }`. |
| `timestamp` | `string` | No | When the event happened, ISO 8601. Left out, Segment uses the time it received the call. Use it when you send historical data. |
| `messageId` | `string` | No | A unique id for the message; Segment drops repeats of the same messageId. Left out, Segment makes one. |
| `traits` | `object` | No | What you know about the user: email, name, plan, createdAt and any custom traits. |

### track

`tracking.track`

Track an event. Records an action the user performed, with properties describing it ("Order Completed" with revenue). Tracking API: sent with the source write key. Events go on to every destination connected to the source.

```ts
});
```

| `timestamp` | `string` | No | When the event happened, ISO 8601. Left out, Segment uses the time it received the call. Use it when you send historical data. |
| `messageId` | `string` | No | A unique id for the message; Segment drops repeats of the same messageId. Left out, Segment makes one. |
| `event` | `string` | Yes | The name of the action, such as "Order Completed". |

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `trackingPlanId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `sourceId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the Source associated with the Tracking Plan. Config API note: analogous to `sourceName`. |

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Connects a Source to a Tracking Plan. |

```ts
} | null
```

## Transformations

### create

`transformations.create`

Create Transformation. Creates a new Transformation. In order to successfully call this endpoint, the specified Workspace needs to have the Protocols feature enabled.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.transformations.create({
	name: 'example',
	sourceId: 'qQEHquLrjRDN9j1ByrChyn',
	enabled: true,
	if: 'example',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The name of the Transformation. |
| `sourceId` | `string` | Yes | The Source to be associated with the Transformation. |
| `destinationMetadataId` | `string` | No | The optional Destination metadata id to be associated with the Transformation. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | Yes | If the Transformation should be enabled. |
| `if` | `string` | Yes | If statement ([FQL](https://segment.com/docs/config-api/fql/)) to match events. For standard event matchers, use the following: Track -\\&gt; "event='\\&lt;eventName\\&gt;'" Identify -\\&gt; "type='identify'" Group -\\&gt; "type='group'" |
| `drop` | `boolean` | No | Optional boolean value if the Transformation should drop the event entirely when the if statement matches, ignores all other transforms. |
| `newEventName` | `string` | No | Optional new event name for renaming events. Works only for 'track' event type. |
| `propertyRenames` | `object[]` | No | Optional array for renaming properties collected by your events. |
| `propertyValueTransformations` | `object[]` | No | Optional array for transforming properties and values collected by your events. Limited to 10 properties. |
| `fqlDefinedProperties` | `object[]` | No | Optional array for defining new properties in [FQL](https://segment.com/docs/config-api/fql/). Currently limited to 1 property. |
| `allowProperties` | `string[]` | No | Optional array for allowing properties from your events. |
| `hashPropertiesConfiguration` | `object` | No | Optional object for hashing properties within an event. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="propertyRenames full type">

```ts
{
  oldName: string,
  newName: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="propertyValueTransformations full type">

```ts
{
  propertyPaths: string[],
  propertyValue: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="fqlDefinedProperties full type">

```ts
{
  fql: string,
  propertyName: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="hashPropertiesConfiguration full type">

```ts
{
  algorithm: string,
  key?: string,
  encoding?: BASE16 | BASE64 | BASE64URL | HEX,
  paths: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | The output of a created Transformation. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  transformation?: {
    id?: string | null,
    name?: string | null,
    sourceId?: string | null,
    destinationMetadataId?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    if?: string | null,
    drop?: boolean | null,
    newEventName?: string | null,
    propertyRenames?: {
      oldName?: string | null,
      newName?: string | null
    }[] | null,
    propertyValueTransformations?: {
      propertyPaths?: string[] | null,
      propertyValue?: string | null
    }[] | null,
    fqlDefinedProperties?: {
      fql?: string | null,
      propertyName?: string | null
    }[] | null,
    allowProperties?: string[] | null,
    hashPropertiesConfiguration?: {
      algorithm?: string | null,
      key?: string | null,
      encoding?: string | null,
      paths?: string[] | null
    } | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### update

`warehouses.update`

Update Warehouse. Updates an existing Warehouse. When called, this endpoint may generate one or more of the following audit trail events:* Storage Destination Modified. * Storage Destination Enabled.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.segment.api.warehouses.update({ warehouseId: 'mKhHAyg5zHF7RstKfQ2Gnr' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `warehouseId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `name` | `string` | No | An optional human-readable name to associate with this Warehouse. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Enable to allow this Warehouse to receive data. |
| `credentialId` | `string` | No | The id of a Credential to connect this Warehouse to. Swapping the Credential re-derives this Warehouse's connection settings and re-validates the connection. The Credential must be the same type as the Warehouse's existing type — a Warehouse's type cannot be changed after creation, so a mismatched Credential is rejected rather than silently ignored. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Returns the updated Warehouse. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  warehouse?: {
    id?: string | null,
    workspaceId?: string | null,
    name?: string | null,
    enabled?: boolean | null,
    credentialId?: string | null,
    settings?: {
    } | null,
    createdAt?: string | null | null,
    updatedAt?: string | null | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>
